\isatagdocument
%
\isamarkupsubsection{Variable relation%
}
\isamarkuptrue%
%
\endisatagdocument
{\isafolddocument}%
%
\isadelimdocument
%
\endisadelimdocument
%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%
One of the main components of relating a non-passified with a passified expression is a variable
relatiion that is a mapping between variables in the non-passified and in the passified program 
(which evolves as two corresponding executions proceed in both programs). Since Boogie performs 
constant propagation as part of the passification, a variable in the non-passified program may 
correspond to a literal value (integer or boolean) in the passified program%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
\isakeywordONE{type{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}synonym}\isamarkupfalse%
\ passive{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}rel\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ {\isachardoublequoteopen}vname\ {\isasymrightharpoonup}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}vname\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ lit{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}{\isachardoublequoteclose}%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%
\isa{\isatconst{passive{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}rel}} models the type of a variable relation%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
%
\begin{isamarkuptext}%
In the following we define some properties on variable relations that we use in our proofs.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

and passive states (usually \isa{\isafree{ns{\isadigit{2}}}}) that are parametrized by variable relations.%

\isamarkupsubsection{Expression relation%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
In the following, we define definitions and lemmas to help relate expressions in the non-passified
and passified programs. \isa{\isafree{e{\isadigit{1}}}} and \isa{\isafree{e{\isadigit{2}}}} usually represent non-passive and passive expressions
respectively.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
\isa{\isaconst{push{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}old{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}expr}} pushes "Old" as far as possible inside. We will use this later to relate an
expression in the non-passified program (which may contain old expressions) with a passified expression.
It will allow us to only have to relate expressions of the form "Old(Var x)" with "Var y".%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

the same value.%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The following inductive definition relates non-passive with passive commands%
